README.md:65:Previous versions of GPGPU-Sim (3.2.0 to 4.1.0) included the [GPUWattch Energy model](http://gpgpu-sim.org/gpuwattch/) which has been replaced by AccelWattch version 1.0 in GPGPU-Sim version 4.2.0. AccelWattch supports modern GPUs and is validated against a NVIDIA Volta QV100 GPU. Detailed documentation on AccelWattch can be found here: [AccelWattch Overview](https://github.com/VijayKandiah/accel-sim-framework#accelwattch-overview) and [AccelWattch MICRO'21 Artifact Manual](https://github.com/VijayKandiah/accel-sim-framework/blob/release/AccelWattch.md).
